\displaystyle\quad-2\theta^{BC}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\theta_{BC}+\theta\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\theta+4\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}^{B}-2\theta_{A}^{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\theta\Big)+\frac{b}{24\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}\Big[-24(\kappa^{rr})^{2}+12\kappa^{B}_{A}\kappa^{A}_{B}

\displaystyle\quad-24\kappa_{A}^{r}\kappa^{rA}+8\kappa^{rr}\kappa_{A}^{A}-4(\kappa_{A}^{A})^{2}+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}\Big(288\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}+24\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}^{A}+15\theta_{B}^{A}\theta_{A}^{B}-9\theta^{2}+72\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}

\displaystyle\quad+12\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{AB}-3\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\theta\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\theta_{A}^{B}+3\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\theta_{BC}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\theta^{BC}\Big)\Big]\,,

(176)

\displaystyle\delta_{\xi}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\pi$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{rA}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}{2}\Big(3\theta_{B}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}T-\theta\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}T\Big)-2\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}T^{(1)}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}{4}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}b\Big(4\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}^{A}+\theta\theta_{B}^{A}-4\theta_{B}^{C}\theta_{C}^{A}+2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}-2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\Big)+\frac{b}{12\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}\Big[-16\kappa^{rr}\kappa^{rA}

\displaystyle\quad+8\kappa_{B}^{B}\kappa^{rA}-24\kappa^{rB}\kappa_{B}^{A}+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}\Big(12\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}+12\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{AB}-3\theta_{B}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}\theta+6\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\,$\kern 0.0pt}}}\lambda^{A}+6\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}

\displaystyle\quad-36\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}-12\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}^{B}+3\theta_{C}^{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\theta_{B}^{C}-12\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}\Big)\Big]\,,

(177)

\displaystyle\delta_{\xi}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\pi$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{AB}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\Big(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}T^{(1)}-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{AB}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}\,T^{(1)}\Big)+\frac{\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}{2}\Big[2\theta^{AB}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}\,T-2\theta^{AC}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{C}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}T

\displaystyle\quad-2\theta^{BC}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{C}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}T+\theta\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}T-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\theta_{C}^{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{C}T+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{AB}\Big(2\theta_{AB}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}T

\displaystyle\quad-\theta\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}\,T-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\theta\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}T\Big)\Big]+\frac{b}{12\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}\Big[-24\kappa^{rA}\kappa^{rB}+8\kappa^{rr}\kappa^{AB}+8\kappa\kappa^{AB}

\displaystyle\quad-24\kappa_{C}^{A}\kappa^{CB}+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}\Big(24\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{AB}+12\theta\theta^{AB}-18\theta_{C}^{A}\theta^{CB}+6\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}\,\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{AB}-6\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{C}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{BC}

\displaystyle\quad-6\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{C}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{AC}+3\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{C}\theta\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\theta^{BC}-6\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}+12\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}

\displaystyle\quad+6\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}-3\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\theta^{CD}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}\theta_{CD}\Big)+\frac{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{AB}}{2}\Big(8(\kappa^{rr})^{2}+24\kappa^{rC}\kappa_{C}^{r}-8\kappa^{rr}\kappa

\displaystyle\quad+12\kappa_{CD}\kappa^{CD}-4\kappa^{2}+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}(-24\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}+21\theta_{CD}\theta^{CD}-9\theta^{2}+24\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}-24\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}\,\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}

\displaystyle\quad+12\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{C}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{D}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{CD}-12\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}\,\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}-3\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{C}\theta\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{C}\theta+3\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{C}\theta_{DF}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{C}\theta^{DF})\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}{4}\partial_{C}b\Big[2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{C}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{AB}-2\theta^{AB}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{C}\theta-2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{BC}-2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{AC}+2\theta_{D}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{D}\theta^{BC}

\displaystyle\quad+2\theta_{D}^{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{D}\theta^{AC}-\theta\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\theta^{BC}+2\theta^{CD}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\theta_{D}^{B}+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{AB}\Big(4\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{C}-4\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{C}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}-2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{C}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}

\displaystyle\quad-\theta_{F}^{D}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{C}\theta_{D}^{F}+\theta\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{C}\theta+4\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{D}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{CD}-2\theta_{D}^{C}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{D}\theta\Big)\Big]\,.

(178)

The transformations
\delta_{\xi}\kappa^{ij}
induce the following transformation law for
V

\delta_{\xi}V=-T+\frac{1}{2}\partial_{A}b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}U+\frac{1}{2}bU\,,

(179)

which, similarly to
\delta_{\xi}U
, can also differ by terms in the kernel of the operator that links
\kappa^{ij}
and
V
, namely, an arbitrary constant.

Now, as mentioned above, if one computes the variation of
g_{rA}
, one finds that it contains a
\mathcal{O}(1)
term, in contradiction with the assumed boundary conditions. In order to preserve the fall-off of the mixed radial-angular
component of the metric one must therefore perform a correcting spatial diffeomorphism
proporitonal to the Lorentz boost parameter

\xi^{r}=0\qquad\text{and$\qquad$}\xi^{A}=\frac{1}{r}I_{(b)}^{A}\,,

(180)

where

I^{A}_{(b)}=\frac{2b}{\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}\kappa^{rA}=2b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}V\,.

(181)

This contribution is part of the “more”-term in (
39
). It only affects the leading order
\theta_{AB}
and the subleading
orders
\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{AB}
of the metric, as well as the subleading orders of the conjugate momentum. Explicitly,
the variation of
\theta_{AB}
acquires the extra contribution

\frac{2}{\sqrt{\overline{g}}}\overline{D}_{A}\left(b\kappa_{B}^{r}\right)+\frac{2}{\sqrt{\overline{g}}}\overline{D}_{B}\left(b\kappa_{A}^{r}\right)\,,

(182)

that of
\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{AB}
is obtained by replacing
\mathcal{L}_{I}\theta_{AB}
by
\mathcal{L}_{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 0.90417pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$I$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\theta_{AB}
where
\mathcal{L}_{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 0.90417pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$I$\kern 0.0pt}}}}
stands for the Lie derivative along
the vector
\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$I$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}=\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}W+I_{(b)}^{A}
, with similar replacements in the variations of
\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\pi$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{ij}
(
\mathcal{L}_{I}\kappa^{ij}\rightarrow\mathcal{L}_{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 0.90417pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$I$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\kappa^{ij}
).
The final form of the transformations will be shown explicitly in
Subsection
B
together with all the other contributions coming from the additional correcting gauge transformations.

Appendix B
Action of the diffeomorphisms on the fields (leading orders, improved)

We now write down explicitly the transformations of the leading orders of the metric and its conjugate momentum, collecting all correction terms, i.e., including the “more” terms in (
37
)-(
39
).

The correcting gauge transformations can most simply be displayed by shifting
the gauge parameters in the following way

\displaystyle\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$I$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}

\displaystyle=\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}W+I_{(b)}^{A}\,,

(183)

T

\displaystyle=T+T_{(b)}\,,

(184)

\displaystyle\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$T$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=T^{(1)}+T_{(b,W)}^{(1)}\,,

(185)

\displaystyle\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$I$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{(1)A}

\displaystyle=I^{(1)A}+I_{(b,W)}^{(1)A}\,,

(186)

with
I_{(b)}^{A}
,
T_{(b)}
,
T_{(b,W)}^{(1)}
and
I_{(b,W)}^{(1)A}

given above.

This directly leads to the following somewhat cumbersome formulas, which play a central role in the derivation of the Poisson bracket of the generators.

Metric

•

Leading order

\displaystyle\delta_{\xi,\xi^{i}}\theta_{AB}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{L}_{Y}\theta_{AB}+2\left(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}W+\overline{g}_{AB}W\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2b}{\sqrt{\overline{g}}}\left(\kappa_{AB}-\frac{1}{2}\overline{g}_{AB}\kappa_{C}^{C}\right)+\frac{2}{\sqrt{\overline{g}}}\overline{D}_{A}\left(b\kappa_{B}^{r}\right)+\frac{2}{\sqrt{\overline{g}}}\overline{D}_{B}\left(b\kappa_{A}^{r}\right)\,.

(187)

From
\delta_{\xi}\theta_{AB}
we can read the transformation law for the field
U
, which is

\delta_{\xi,\xi^{i}}U=Y^{A}\partial_{A}U+2W+2bV\,.

(188)

As mentioned above, an arbitrary term of the form
\Sigma_{\ell,m}\alpha_{\ell,m}Y^{1\ell m}
(spanning the kernel of the operator
\overline{D}_{A}\overline{D}_{B}+\overline{g}_{AB}
) can be added to the transformation law of
U
. This arbitrariness drops from the formulas giving the charges, as it should.

•

First subleading order (“core”)

\displaystyle\delta_{\xi,\xi^{i}}\overline{\lambda}

\displaystyle=Y^{A}\partial_{A}\overline{\lambda}-W^{(1)}+\frac{b}{3\sqrt{\overline{g}}}\left(2\overline{\pi}^{rr}-\overline{\pi}_{A}^{A}-\theta_{AB}\kappa^{AB}\right)\,,

(189)

\displaystyle\delta_{\xi,\xi^{i}}\overline{\lambda}_{A}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{L}_{Y}\overline{\lambda}_{A}+\overline{D}_{A}W^{(1)}-2I_{A}^{(1)}-\frac{2b}{\sqrt{\overline{g}}}\left(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\pi$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}^{r}+\theta_{B}^{A}\kappa^{rB}+\theta_{B}^{B}\kappa_{A}^{r}\right)+\frac{2T}{\sqrt{\overline{g}}}\kappa_{A}^{r}\,,

(190)

\displaystyle\delta_{\xi,\xi^{i}}\overline{h}_{AB}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{L}_{Y}\overline{h}_{AB}+\mathcal{L}_{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 0.90417pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$I$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\theta_{AB}+W\theta_{AB}+2\left(\overline{D}_{(A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$I$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B)}^{(1)}+\overline{g}_{AB}W^{(1)}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2b}{\sqrt{\overline{g}}}\left[\overline{\pi}_{AB}+2\theta_{C(A}\kappa_{B)}^{C}-\frac{1}{2}\theta_{AB}\kappa_{C}^{C}-\frac{1}{3}\overline{g}_{AB}\left(\overline{\pi}^{rr}+\overline{\pi}_{C}^{C}+\theta_{CD}\kappa^{CD}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{2b}{\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}\theta_{D}^{D}\left(\kappa_{AB}-\frac{1}{2}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{AB}\kappa_{C}^{C}\right)+\frac{2T}{\sqrt{\overline{g}}}\left(\kappa_{AB}-\frac{1}{2}\overline{g}_{AB}\kappa_{C}^{C}\right)\,.

(191)

where
\mathcal{L}_{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 0.90417pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$I$\kern 0.0pt}}}}
stands for the Lie derivative along
the vector
\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$I$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}=\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}W+I_{(b)}^{A}
.

Conjugate momentum

•

Leading order

\displaystyle\delta_{\xi,\xi^{i}}\kappa^{rr}

\displaystyle=\partial_{A}\left(Y^{A}\kappa^{rr}\right)-\frac{\sqrt{\overline{g}}}{2}\left(4b\theta_{A}^{A}-3\partial_{A}b\overline{D}^{A}\theta_{B}^{B}-b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}\theta_{A}^{A}\right)-\sqrt{\overline{g}}\,\overline{\triangle}T\,,

(192)

\displaystyle\delta_{\xi,\xi^{i}}\kappa^{rA}

\displaystyle=\partial_{B}\left(Y^{B}\kappa^{rA}\right)-\partial_{B}Y^{A}\kappa^{rB}+\frac{\sqrt{\overline{g}}}{2}\left[\partial_{B}b\theta^{AB}+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\left(b\theta_{B}^{B}\right)\right]-\sqrt{\overline{g}}\,\overline{D}^{A}T\,,

(193)

\displaystyle\delta_{\xi,\xi^{i}}\kappa^{AB}

\displaystyle=\partial_{C}\left(Y^{C}\kappa^{AB}\right)-\partial_{C}Y^{A}\kappa^{CB}-\partial_{C}Y^{A}\kappa^{CA}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sqrt{\overline{g}}}{2}\left[b\theta^{AB}-\partial_{C}b\overline{D}^{B}\theta^{AC}-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{AB}\left(2b\theta_{C}^{C}-3\partial_{C}b\overline{D}^{C}\theta_{D}^{D}-b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}\theta_{C}^{C}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\sqrt{\overline{g}}}{2}\left(2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{(A}b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B)}\theta_{C}^{C}+b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}\theta_{C}^{C}\right)-\sqrt{\overline{g}}\left(\overline{g}^{AB}\overline{\triangle}T-\overline{D}^{A}\overline{D}^{B}T\right)\,.

(194)

From these transformation laws, we obtain that the field
V
must transform as

\delta_{\xi,\xi^{i}}V=Y^{A}\partial_{A}V-T+2bU+\frac{1}{2}\partial_{A}b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}U+\frac{1}{2}b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}U\,,

(195)

an expression to which one can add an arbitrary constant, which parametrizes as we pointed out the kernel of the operator relating
\kappa^{ij}
to
V
, and which again drops from the expression of the charges.

•

First subleading order (“core”)

\displaystyle\delta_{\xi,\xi^{i}}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\pi$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{rr}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{L}_{Y}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\pi$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{rr}+\mathcal{L}_{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 0.90417pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$I$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\kappa^{rr}-2\kappa_{A}^{r}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}W+\kappa^{rr}W-\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\big(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}-3\big)\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$T$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{(1)}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}{2}\big(-\theta\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}\,\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$T$\kern 0.0pt}}}+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\theta\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$T$\kern 0.0pt}}}+2\theta_{B}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$T$\kern 0.0pt}}}\big)+\frac{\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}{4}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}b\Big(12\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}-2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}^{B}

\displaystyle\quad-2\theta^{BC}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\theta_{BC}+\theta\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\theta+4\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}^{B}-2\theta_{A}^{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\theta\Big)+\frac{b}{24\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}\Big[-24(\kappa^{rr})^{2}+12\kappa^{B}_{A}\kappa^{A}_{B}

\displaystyle\quad-24\kappa_{A}^{r}\kappa^{rA}+8\kappa^{rr}\kappa_{A}^{A}-4(\kappa_{A}^{A})^{2}+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}\Big(288\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}+24\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}^{A}+15\theta_{B}^{A}\theta_{A}^{B}-9\theta^{2}+72\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}

The transformation laws of the next subleading terms in the asymptotic expansion of the fields can be found in appendix
E
.

Appendix C
Decomposition of the spatial metric and spatial curvature

We denote in this appendix polar coordinates as
x^{i}=(r,x^{A})
,
where
x^{A}
are coordinates on the
3
-sphere. We introduce the “lapse”
\lambda
and the “shift”
\lambda^{A}
adapted to the slicing of space by the spheres of constant radius
r
,

\gamma_{AB}\equiv g_{AB},\quad\lambda_{A}\equiv g_{rA},\quad\lambda\equiv\frac{1}{\sqrt{g^{rr}}}.

(199)

In terms of these, the metric and its inverse take the form:

g_{ij}=\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\lambda^{2}+\lambda_{C}\lambda^{C}&\lambda_{B}\\
\lambda_{A}&\gamma_{AB}\end{array}\right),\quad g^{ij}=\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\frac{1}{\lambda^{2}}&-\frac{\lambda^{B}}{\lambda^{2}}\\
-\frac{\lambda^{A}}{\lambda^{2}}&\gamma^{AB}+\frac{\lambda^{A}\lambda^{B}}{\lambda^{2}}\end{array}\right),

(200)

where we used
\gamma_{AB}
and its inverse
\gamma^{AB}
to raise and
lower the angular indices
A,B,...

Introducing the extrinsic curvature of the 3-spheres
K_{AB}
, we can write
all the Christoffel symbols:

\displaystyle K_{AB}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\lambda}\left(-\partial_{r}g_{AB}+D_{A}\lambda_{B}+D_{B}\lambda_{A}\right)

(201)

\displaystyle\Gamma^{r}_{AB}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda}K_{AB}

(202)

\displaystyle\Gamma^{A}_{BC}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{}^{\gamma}\Gamma^{A}_{BC}-\frac{\lambda^{A}}{\lambda}K_{BC}

(203)

\displaystyle\Gamma^{r}_{rA}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda}\left(\partial_{A}\lambda+K_{AB}\lambda^{B}\right)

(204)

\displaystyle\Gamma^{r}_{rr}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda}\partial_{r}\lambda+\frac{\lambda^{A}}{\lambda}\left(\partial_{A}\lambda+K_{AB}\lambda^{B}\right)

(205)

\displaystyle\Gamma^{A}_{rB}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\lambda^{A}}{\lambda}\left(\partial_{B}\lambda+K_{BC}\lambda^{C}\right)+D_{B}\lambda^{A}-\lambda K^{A}_{B}

(206)

\displaystyle\Gamma^{A}_{rr}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\lambda\left(\gamma^{AB}+\frac{\lambda^{A}\lambda^{B}}{\lambda^{2}}\right)\left(\partial_{B}\lambda+K_{BC}\lambda^{C}\right)-\lambda^{C}\left(D^{A}\lambda_{C}-\lambda K^{A}_{C}\right)

(207)

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{\lambda^{A}}{\lambda}\partial_{r}\lambda+\gamma^{AB}\partial_{r}\lambda_{B}

where
D_{A}
is the covariant derivative associated to
\gamma_{AB}
.The Ricci tensor is given by:

\displaystyle{}^{(4)}R_{AB}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda}\partial_{r}K_{AB}+2K_{AC}K^{C}_{B}-KK_{AB}-\frac{1}{\lambda}D_{A}D_{B}\lambda

(208)

\displaystyle\quad+{}^{\gamma}R_{AB}-\frac{1}{\lambda}\mathcal{L}_{\lambda}K_{AB},

\displaystyle{}^{(4)}R_{rA}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\left(\partial_{A}K-D_{B}K^{B}_{A}\right)+{}^{(4)}R_{AB}\lambda^{B},

(209)

\displaystyle{}^{(4)}R_{rr}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda(\partial_{r}K-\lambda^{A}\partial_{A}K)-\lambda^{2}K^{A}_{B}K^{B}_{A}-\lambda D_{A}D^{A}\lambda

(210)

\displaystyle\quad-{}^{(4)}R_{AB}\lambda^{A}\lambda^{B}+2\,{}^{(4)}R_{rB}\lambda^{B},

which implies that the Ricci scalar takes the form

{}^{(4)}R=\frac{2}{\lambda}(\partial_{r}K-\lambda^{A}\partial_{A}K)+{}^{\gamma}R-K^{A}_{B}K^{B}_{A}-K^{2}-\frac{2}{\lambda}D_{A}D^{A}\lambda.

(211)

Appendix D
Vanishing of the linear divergence in the canonical generator

In this appendix we will explicitly show that the linear divergence of the canonical generator

\displaystyle I_{\text{linear}}

\displaystyle=\oint d^{3}x\Big[\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}b\left(2\delta\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$k$\kern 0.0pt}}}+\frac{1}{2}\theta\delta\theta+\frac{1}{2}\theta_{AB}\delta\theta^{AB}\right)+\frac{4b}{\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}\kappa^{r}_{A}\delta\kappa^{rA}

(212)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+2Y^{A}\delta\left(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\pi$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{r}_{A}+\theta_{AB}\kappa^{rB}\right)+2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}W\delta\kappa^{r}_{A}+2W\delta\kappa^{rr}\Big]\,,

(213)

vanishes by virtue of the faster fall-off of the constraint equations. In fact, by using the fact that
\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\kappa^{rA}-\kappa^{rr}=0
, we can see that the two last terms terms in (
213
) cancel each other. For the remaining terms of (
213
), we make use of the faster fall-off of the radial component of momentum constraint
\mathcal{H}_{A}\sim\mathcal{O}\left(r^{-1}\right)
, which implies that

\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\pi$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}_{A}+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{AB}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\pi$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{rB}-\theta_{AB}\kappa^{rB}+\frac{1}{2}(2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\theta_{AC}-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\theta_{BC})\kappa^{BC}=0\,.

(214)

Then, we have that

\oint d^{3}xY^{A}\delta\left(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\pi$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{r}_{A}+\theta_{AB}\kappa^{rB}\right)=\delta\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\left(Y^{A}\theta_{AB}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}V-\frac{1}{2}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}Y^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{C}U\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{C}V\right)\,.

(215)

It can be shown this last integral vanishes (after some integration by parts) by virtue of the following properties of the Killing vector
Y^{A}
:

\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}Y^{B}=\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{[A}Y^{B]}\,,\quad\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}Y_{C}=-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{AB}Y_{C}+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{AC}Y_{B}\,.

(216)

The remaining terms in (
212
) along the boost parameter
b
become

\delta\oint d^{3}x\,\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,b\Big(2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}+6\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}+\frac{1}{4}\theta^{2}-\frac{3}{4}\theta^{A}_{B}\theta^{B}_{A}+2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}V\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}V+2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\Big).

(217)

Using the properties of
b
, we have

\delta\oint d^{3}x\,\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,b\Big(2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}+6\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}\Big)=\delta\oint d^{3}x\,\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,b\Big(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{AB}-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}-2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}\Big)\,.

(218)

Using the asymptotic constraint coming from the fact that
\mathcal{H}\sim\mathcal{O}\left(r^{-2}\right)
, we have that

\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{AB}-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}})-2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}+2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\\
=2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}V\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}V+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}V\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}V-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}V\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}V\\
+\frac{1}{4}\theta^{2}-\frac{3}{4}\theta^{AB}\theta_{AB}-\frac{1}{4}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}\theta^{AC}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\theta_{BC}+\frac{1}{4}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\theta^{AC}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}\theta_{BC},

(219)

the boundary term then reduces to two independent terms which are both zero:

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{1}

\displaystyle=\delta\oint d^{3}x\,\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,b\Big(\frac{1}{2}\theta^{2}-\frac{3}{2}\theta^{A}_{B}\theta^{B}_{A}-\frac{1}{4}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}\theta^{AC}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\theta_{BC}+\frac{1}{4}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\theta^{AC}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}\theta_{BC}\Big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4}\delta\oint d^{3}x\,\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}\Big(b(-\theta^{AC}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\theta_{BC}+\theta^{C}_{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\theta_{AC})\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{4}\delta\oint d^{3}x\,\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\left\{b\Big(\theta^{2}-3\theta^{A}_{B}\theta^{B}_{A}\Big)+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}b\Big(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}(\theta^{AC}\theta_{AC})-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}(\theta^{C}_{B}\theta_{AC})\Big)\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4}\delta\oint d^{3}x\,\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}\Big(b(-\theta^{AC}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\theta_{BC}+\theta^{C}_{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\theta_{AC})+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}b\,\theta^{AC}\theta_{AC}-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}b\,\theta^{A}_{C}\theta^{C}_{B}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{4}\delta\oint d^{3}x\,\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,b\Big(\theta^{2}-\theta^{A}_{B}\theta^{B}_{A}\Big)=0,

(220)

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{2}

\displaystyle=\delta\oint d^{3}x\,\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,b\Big(4\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}V\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}V+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}V\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}V-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}V\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}V\Big)

\displaystyle=\delta\oint d^{3}x\,\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\Big((b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}V-V\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}b)(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}V-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\gamma$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{AB}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{C}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{C}V)\Big)=0,

(221)

where we have used

b(\theta^{2}-\theta^{A}_{B}\theta^{B}_{A})=-b(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}U\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}U-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}U\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}U)+4bU\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}U+6bU^{2}\\
=\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\Big((b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}U-U\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}b)\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}U-(b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}U-U\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}b)\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}U+b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}U^{2}-2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}bU^{2}\Big).

(222)

Appendix E
Transformation laws of the subleading terms

In this appendix, we list the transformation laws of the subleading terms of the fields. These are necessary to obtain the asymptotic symmetry algebra. The subleading components of the metric fall-off transform as follows

\displaystyle\delta_{\xi,\xi^{i}}h^{(2)}_{rr}

\displaystyle=Y^{A}\partial_{A}h^{(2)}_{rr}-2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}W\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}+2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}W\partial_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}-4W\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}+\frac{2T}{3\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}\Big(2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\pi$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{rr}-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\pi$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}_{A}-\theta_{AB}\kappa^{AB}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{2b}{3\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}\theta\Big(2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\pi$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{rr}-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\pi$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}_{A}-\theta_{AB}\kappa^{AB}\Big)+\frac{2b}{3\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}\Big(2\pi^{(2)rr}-\pi^{(2)A}_{A}+4\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}\,\kappa^{rr}+6\partial_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}\kappa^{rA}

\displaystyle\quad-2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\kappa^{rA}-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{AB}\kappa^{AB}-\theta_{AB}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\pi$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{AB}\Big)\,,

(223)

\displaystyle\delta_{\xi,\xi^{i}}h^{(2)}_{rA}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{L}_{Y}h^{(2)}_{rA}-2I^{(1)B}\theta_{AB}-W\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}+2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}\partial_{A}W+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\lambda$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}W-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$h$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}^{B}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}W+\theta_{A}^{B}\theta_{B}^{C}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{C}W

Appendix F
An obstruction to the elimination of the non-linear terms in the algebra

We have suceeded in the main text in redefining away the non-linearities appearing in the BMS
5
algebra, except for the brackets
between boosts and leading supertranslations. Specifically, these brackets
read

\displaystyle\{Q_{b},Q_{T}\}

\displaystyle=Q_{\hat{W}}+\Lambda_{\{b,T\}}\,,

(231)

\displaystyle\{Q_{b},Q_{W}\}

\displaystyle=Q_{\hat{T}}+\Lambda_{\{b,W\}}\,,

(232)

where

\hat{W}=-bT\,,\quad\hat{T}=-4bW-\partial_{A}b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}W-b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}W\,,

(233)

and where the non-linear terms are given by

\displaystyle\Lambda_{\{b,T\}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\oint d^{3}xb\kappa^{rA}\left(\theta_{A}^{B}\partial_{B}T-\theta\partial_{A}T\right)\,,

(234)

\displaystyle\Lambda_{\{b,W\}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\oint d^{3}x\frac{1}{\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}\Big[bW(2\kappa^{rA}\kappa_{A}^{r}-2\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\kappa^{rA}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\kappa^{rB}+\kappa^{AB}\kappa_{AB})+\partial_{A}b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}W\kappa^{rA}\kappa_{A}^{r}

(235)

\displaystyle+4b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}W\kappa^{rA}\kappa_{A}^{r}-4b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}W\big(\kappa_{AB}\kappa^{rB}-\kappa_{B}^{r}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\kappa^{rB}+\kappa^{rB}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\kappa_{A}^{r}-\frac{1}{2}\kappa_{A}^{r}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\kappa^{rB}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle+\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,\Big[\frac{1}{4}bW\big(5\theta^{2}-7\theta_{AB}\theta^{AB}+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\theta\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\theta-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\theta_{BC}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\theta^{BC}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}W\big(\theta_{AB}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}\theta-2\theta^{BC}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\theta_{BC}\big)-\frac{1}{4}\partial_{A}b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}W\theta_{BC}\theta^{BC}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}W\big(2\theta^{2}-3\theta_{BC}\theta^{BC}\big)+\frac{1}{2}b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}W\big(\theta_{A}^{C}\theta_{CB}-\theta_{AB}\theta\big)\Big]\,.

The problem of linearizing a Poisson structure can be reformulated as a problem of Lie algebra cohomology
Weinstein
(see review in the paper
FernandesMonnier
). In our case, the algebra is the semi-direct product of the homogeneous Lorentz algebra with its infinite-dimensional representation spanned by the supertranslations, which is non semi-simple. The cohomology is therefore expected to be non-trivial so one cannot invoke general theorems to infer that the non-linearities could be removed.

Since the non-linearities appear only in the brackets between boosts and leading supertranslations, one can reformulate the problem of eliminating them as a problem of Lie agebra cohomology for the finite-dimensional Lorentz subalgebra in the representation given by the supertranslations. Because this representation is infinite-dimensional (and reducible but indecomposable), however, this does not provide much insight. Furthermore, locality requirements on the form of the generators must be preserved.

Given the intricacy of this question, we shall examine it in a restricted context, namely, we shall only consider redefinitions of the supertranslation charges that take the form

\displaystyle\tilde{Q}_{T}

\displaystyle=Q_{T}+\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,TF\,,

(236)

\displaystyle\tilde{Q}_{W}

\displaystyle=Q_{W}+\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,WG\,.

(237)

where the functions
F
and
G
are assumed to be the most general quadratic, rotation-invariant homogeneous polynomials in
\theta^{AB}
and
\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\kappa^{rB}
, i.e.,

\displaystyle F

\displaystyle=A_{1}\theta_{B}^{A}\theta_{A}^{B}+B_{1}\theta^{2}+\frac{C_{1}}{\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\kappa_{B}^{r}\theta^{AB}+\frac{D_{1}}{\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\kappa^{rA}\theta+\frac{F_{1}}{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\kappa^{rA}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\kappa^{rB}+\frac{G_{1}}{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\kappa_{B}^{r}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\kappa^{rB}\,,

(238)

\displaystyle G

\displaystyle=A_{2}\theta_{B}^{A}\theta_{A}^{B}+B_{2}\theta^{2}+\frac{C_{2}}{\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\kappa_{B}^{r}\theta^{AB}+\frac{D_{2}}{\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\kappa^{rA}\theta+\frac{F_{2}}{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\kappa^{rA}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\kappa^{rB}+\frac{G_{2}}{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\kappa_{B}^{r}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\kappa^{rB}\,.

(239)

Here
A_{i}
,
B_{i}
,
C_{i}
,
D_{i}
,
F_{i}
and
G_{i}
are at this stage arbitrary constants. We consider quadratic polynomials because the non-linearities that we want to eliminate are themselves quadratic, and the number of derivatives is also taken to match.

One can view the new terms as generating improper gauge transformations with parameters

\displaystyle T_{T}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=-A_{1}\left(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}+3\right)^{-1}\left[\left(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{AB}\right)\left(2T\theta_{AB}\right)\right]-2B_{1}T\theta

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{C_{1}}{\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}\left(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}+3\right)^{-1}\left[\left(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{AB}\right)\left(T\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\kappa_{B}^{r}\right)\right]-\frac{D_{1}}{\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}T\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\kappa^{rA}\,,

(240)

\displaystyle I_{T}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\left[-\frac{1}{2}C_{1}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\left(T\theta^{AB}\right)-\frac{1}{2}D_{1}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\left(T\theta\right)-\frac{F_{1}}{\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\left(T\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\kappa^{rB}\right)-\frac{G_{1}}{\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\left(T\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}\kappa^{rA}\right)\right]\,,

(241)

for
F
and

\displaystyle T_{W}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=-A_{2}\left(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}+3\right)^{-1}\left[\left(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{AB}\right)\left(2W\theta_{AB}\right)\right]-2B_{2}W\theta

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{C_{2}}{\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}\left(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}+3\right)^{-1}\left[\left(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{AB}\right)\left(W\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\kappa_{B}^{r}\right)\right]-\frac{D_{2}}{\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}W\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\kappa^{rA}\,,

(242)

\displaystyle I_{W}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\left[-\frac{1}{2}C_{2}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\left(W\theta^{AB}\right)-\frac{1}{2}D_{2}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\left(W\theta\right)-\frac{F_{2}}{\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\left(W\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\kappa^{rB}\right)-\frac{G_{2}}{\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}\left(W\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}\kappa^{rA}\right)\right]\,.

(243)

for
G
.
Of course, the surface integrals are accompanied by the appropriate bulk terms proportional to the constraints.

The non-linearities cannot be eliminated

We now investigate whether it is possible to determine the arbitrary coefficients in
F
and
G
in such a way that

\displaystyle\delta_{b}\tilde{Q}_{T}

\displaystyle=\tilde{Q}_{\hat{W}}\,,

(244)

\displaystyle\delta_{b}\tilde{Q}_{W}

\displaystyle=\tilde{Q}_{\hat{T}}\,,

(245)

where
\hat{W}=bT
and
\hat{T}=4bW+\partial_{A}b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}W+b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}W
.

We start taking the transformation of
\tilde{Q}_{T}
under Lorentz
boosts

\displaystyle\delta_{b}\tilde{Q}_{T}

\displaystyle=\delta_{b}Q_{T}+\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,T\delta_{b}F

(246)

\displaystyle=Q_{\hat{W}}+\Lambda_{\{T,b\}}+\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,T\delta_{b}F

(247)

\displaystyle=\tilde{Q}_{\hat{W}}-\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,\hat{W}G+\Lambda_{\{T,b\}}+\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,T\delta_{b}F\,,

(248)

where the relation
\delta_{b}Q_{T}=Q_{\hat{W}}+\Lambda_{\{T,b\}}
was used.
Note that it is possible to write the non-linear term as

\Lambda_{\{T,b\}}=-\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,T\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}f^{A}\,,

(249)

with
f^{A}=b\left(\kappa^{rB}\theta_{B}^{A}-\kappa^{rA}\theta\right)
.
Then, if we demand (
244
), we obtain the first condition
on the functions
F
and G:

\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,T\left(\delta_{b}F-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}f^{A}-bG\right)=0\,.

(250)

We now proceed similarly for
\tilde{Q}_{W}
:

\displaystyle\delta_{b}\tilde{Q}_{W}

\displaystyle=\delta_{b}Q_{W}+\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,W\delta_{b}G

(251)

\displaystyle=Q_{\hat{T}}+\Lambda_{\{W,b\}}+\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,W\delta_{b}G

(252)

\displaystyle=\tilde{Q}_{\hat{T}}-\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,\hat{T}F+\Lambda_{\{W,b\}}+\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,W\delta_{b}G\,,

(253)

where the relation
\delta_{b}Q_{W}=Q_{\hat{T}}+\Lambda_{\{W,b\}}
was used.
Note that the non-linear term in this case can be written as

\Lambda_{\{W,b\}}=\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,W\left(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{AB}\right)f^{AB}\,,

(254)

with

f^{AB}=-b\Big[\frac{3}{4}\theta^{A}_{C}\theta^{BC}-\theta^{AB}\theta+6V\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}V+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{AB}\Big(\frac{3}{4}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\theta^{D}_{C}\theta^{C}_{D}+V^{2}+V\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}V-\frac{7}{2}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{C}V\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{C}V\Big)\Big]\,.

(255)

Thus, if we now demand (
245
), we obtain the second condition

\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,W\left[\delta_{b}G+\left(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{AB}\right)f^{AB}-4bF-\partial_{A}b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}F-b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}F\right]=0\,.

(256)

In summary, we have that the two following conditions must be simultaneously
satisfied

\displaystyle\delta_{b}F-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}f^{A}-bG

\displaystyle=0\,,

(257)

\displaystyle\delta_{b}G+\left(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{AB}\right)f^{AB}-4bF-\partial_{A}b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}F-b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}F

\displaystyle=0\,.

(258)

Now, if we solve the first condition, we find upon computing
\delta_{b}F
and
\delta_{b}G
from
\delta_{b}\theta_{AB}
and
\delta_{b}\kappa^{rA}
given in Appendix
B
, that the coefficients
A_{i}
,
B_{i}
,
C_{i}
,
D_{i}
,
F_{i}
and
G_{i}
are completely determined. The only non-vanishing
ones are

A_{1}=\frac{1}{8}\,,\quad B_{1}=-\frac{1}{8}\,,\quad C_{2}=-\frac{1}{2}\,,\quad D_{2}=\frac{1}{2}\,.

(259)

This yields

\displaystyle\tilde{Q}_{T}

\displaystyle=Q_{T}+\frac{1}{8}\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,T\left(\theta^{AB}\theta_{AB}-\theta^{2}\right)\,,

(260)

\displaystyle\tilde{Q}_{W}

\displaystyle=Q_{W}-\frac{1}{2}\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,W\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\kappa_{B}^{r}\left(\theta^{AB}-\theta\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{AB}\right)\,.

(261)

The bracket with the boosts now read

\displaystyle\{\tilde{Q}_{b},\tilde{Q}_{T}\}

\displaystyle=\tilde{Q}_{\hat{W}}\,,

(262)

\displaystyle\{\tilde{Q}_{b},\tilde{Q}_{W}\}

\displaystyle=\tilde{Q}_{\hat{T}}+\tilde{\Lambda}_{\{b,W\}}\,,

(263)

with

\displaystyle\tilde{\Lambda}_{\{b,W\}}

\displaystyle=-2\oint d^{3}x\frac{bW}{\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}}\kappa^{rA}\kappa_{A}^{r}+\tilde{\Lambda}_{\{b,W\}}^{\theta}\,.

(264)

Here,

\displaystyle\tilde{\Lambda}_{\{b,W\}}^{\theta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,\Big[\frac{1}{4}bW\big(5\theta^{2}-7\theta_{AB}\theta^{AB}+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\theta\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\theta-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\theta_{BC}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\theta^{BC}\big)

(265)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}W\big(\theta_{AB}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}\theta-2\theta^{BC}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\theta_{BC}\big)-\frac{1}{4}\partial_{A}b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}W\theta_{BC}\theta^{BC}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}W\big(2\theta^{2}-3\theta_{BC}\theta^{BC}\big)+\frac{1}{2}b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}W\big(\theta_{A}^{C}\theta_{CB}-\theta_{AB}\theta\big)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\left(\partial_{C}b\theta_{B}^{C}+\partial_{B}(b\theta)\right)\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}W\left(\theta^{AB}-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{AB}\theta\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\left(4bW+\partial_{C}b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{C}W+b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}W\right)\left(\theta^{AB}\theta_{AB}-\theta^{2}\right)\Big]\,.

The non-linearity in
\{\tilde{Q}_{b},\tilde{Q}_{W}\}
has not been eliminated. It is therefore not possible to eliminate simultaneously all the non-linearities within the class of redefinitions of the generators that we have considered.

Insight from the Jacobi identity

The above redefinition (
259
) makes at the same time the supertranslations non-abelian.
Indeed, the new supertranslation algebra is given by

\displaystyle\{\tilde{Q}_{T_{1}},\tilde{Q}_{T_{2}}\}

\displaystyle=0\,,

(266)

\displaystyle\{\tilde{Q}_{T},\tilde{Q}_{W}\}

\displaystyle=Q_{\hat{T}^{(1)}}\,,

(267)

\displaystyle\{\tilde{Q}_{W_{1}},\tilde{Q}_{W_{2}}\}

\displaystyle=Q_{\hat{I}^{(1)}}\,,

(268)

where

\displaystyle\hat{T}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}W\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}T+\frac{1}{2}T\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}W+\frac{3}{2}TW

\displaystyle\quad+(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}+3)^{-1}\left[\left(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{B}+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{AB}\right)\left(\frac{1}{2}W\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}T-\frac{1}{2}T(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{B}W+\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{AB}W)\right)\right]\,,

(269)

\displaystyle\hat{I}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=W_{1}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}W_{2}-W_{2}\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}W_{1}\,.

(270)

The presence of the non-abelian terms in the new supertranslation algebra forces in fact the presence of non-linearities in the brackets with the boosts. Indeed, the Jacobi identity would otherwise be violated.

One can for instance check explicitly that the Jacobi identity involving
boosts, time supertranslations and spatial supertranslations,

\{Q_{b},\{\tilde{Q}_{T},\tilde{Q}_{W}\}\}+\{\tilde{Q}_{W},\{Q_{b},\tilde{Q}_{T}\}\}+\{\tilde{Q}_{T},\{\tilde{Q}_{W},Q_{b}\}\}=0\,,

(271)

is satisfied only if the non-linear term
\tilde{\Lambda}_{(W,b)}
is
present. In fact, we have that the first term of the Jacobi identity
reduces to

\{Q_{b},\{\tilde{Q}_{T},\tilde{Q}_{W}\}\}=\{Q_{b},Q_{\hat{T}^{(1)}}\}=Q_{\hat{I}^{(1)}}=2\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,b(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}+3)\hat{T}^{(1)}V\,,

(272)

where we used that
\hat{I}^{(1)}=-b(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}+3)\hat{T}^{(1)}
,
with
\hat{T}^{(1)}
given in (
269
).

The second term in the the Jacobi identity gives

\displaystyle\{\tilde{Q}_{W},\{Q_{b},\tilde{Q}_{T}\}\}

\displaystyle=\{\tilde{Q}_{W},\tilde{Q}_{\hat{W}}\}

(273)

\displaystyle=Q_{\hat{I}^{(1)}}

(274)

\displaystyle=2\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\left(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}W\hat{W}-\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}\hat{W}W\right)V\,,

(275)

\displaystyle=2\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\left[b\left(W\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}T-T\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}W-3TW\right)+2\partial_{A}b\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}TW\right]V

(276)

where we used that
\hat{W}=-bT
and the expression for
\hat{I}^{(1)}

given in (
270
).

Finally, the third term in Jacobi identity is given by

\displaystyle\{\tilde{Q}_{T},\{\tilde{Q}_{W},Q_{b}\}\}

\displaystyle=-\{\tilde{Q}_{T},\tilde{Q}_{\hat{T}}+\tilde{\Lambda}_{\{b,W\}}\}

(277)

\displaystyle=-\{\tilde{Q}_{T},\tilde{\Lambda}_{\{b,W\}}\}

(278)

\displaystyle=4\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}bW\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}_{A}T\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}V

(279)

\displaystyle=-4\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\left(\partial_{A}bW\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}T+b\partial_{A}W\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$D$\kern 0.0pt}}}^{A}T+bW\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}T\right)V\,.

(280)

One can immediately see that the non-linear term
\tilde{\Lambda}_{\{b,W\}}

is needed to eliminate the term along
\partial_{A}b
in (
276
).
Summing over the three components of the Jacobi identity (
271
),
we conclude that it is identically satisfied, as it should, but only because
\tilde{\Lambda}_{(b,W)}
is present.

Abelian supertranslations

As we argued in the text, the condition that the leading supertranslations are abelian plays an important role in the invariance properties of the energy and is thus a desirable feature.
One can ask which values of the coefficients
A_{i}
,
B_{i}
,
C_{i}
,
D_{i}
,
F_{i}
and
G_{i}
preserve this property, i.e., are such that

\delta_{T_{2}}\tilde{Q}_{T_{1}}=\delta_{T}\tilde{Q}_{W}\left(=-\delta_{T}\tilde{Q}_{W}\right)=\delta_{W_{2}}\tilde{Q}_{W_{1}}=0\,.

(281)

A direct computation yields

\displaystyle\delta_{T_{2}}\tilde{Q}_{T_{1}}

\displaystyle=\delta_{I_{T_{2}}^{(1)}}Q_{T}+\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,T_{1}\delta_{T_{2}}F=\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,T_{1}\left(\delta_{T_{2}}F-2I_{T_{2}}^{(1)}\right)=0\,,

(282)

\displaystyle\delta_{W}\tilde{Q}_{T}

\displaystyle=\delta_{I_{W}^{(1)}}Q_{T}+\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,T\delta_{W}F=\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,T\left(\delta_{W}F-2I_{W}^{(1)}\right)=0\,,

(283)

\displaystyle\delta_{T}\tilde{Q}_{W}

\displaystyle=\delta_{T_{T}^{(1)}}Q_{W}+\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,W\delta_{T}G=\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,W\left(\delta_{T}G+2\left(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}+3\right)T_{T}^{(1)}\right)=0\,,

(284)

\displaystyle\delta_{W_{2}}\tilde{Q}_{W_{1}}

\displaystyle=\delta_{T_{W_{2}}^{(1)}}Q_{W_{1}}+\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,W_{1}\delta_{W_{2}}G=\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,W_{1}\left(\delta_{W_{2}}G+2\left(\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$\triangle$\kern 0.0pt}}}+3\right)T_{W_{2}}^{(1)}\right)=0\,.

(285)

Replacing the above parameters into these conditions and using (
240
)-(
243
), we get that the only non-vanishing parameters
turn out to be
A_{2}
and
B_{2}
, subject to the condition

A_{2}+B_{2}=0\,.

(286)

Thus
F=0
and
G=B\left(\theta^{2}-\theta_{AB}\theta^{AB}\right)
with
B
an arbitrary constant, so that the most general abelian supertranslation charges are given
by

\displaystyle\tilde{Q}_{T}

\displaystyle=Q_{T}\,,

(287)

\displaystyle\tilde{Q}_{W}

\displaystyle=Q_{W}+B_{2}\oint d^{3}x\sqrt{\hbox{\vbox{\hrule height=0.5pt\kern 1.29167pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt$g$\kern 0.0pt}}}}\,W\left(\theta^{2}-\theta_{AB}\theta^{AB}\right)\,.

(288)

The abelian condition is therefore rather strong and in any case, incompatible with the elimination on the non-linearities, which needs different values of the coefficients
A_{i}
,
B_{i}
,
C_{i}
,
D_{i}
,
F_{i}
and
G_{i}
.
